Additional mandatory checks: image text correction marks artwork stale; vision validation false-negative requires human rejection. Reorder slide numbering requires regeneration where visible order embedded. Reel storyboard has no Download MP4/playable final video status. Reel audio duration mismatch blocks render until explicit retime; all cues fit validated final duration. Both generates separate outputs/approvals/posts. Approving Carousel never approves Reel; editing Reel caption never invalidates independent Carousel approval. Shared evidence edit invalidates all dependent output approvals. Unknown Instagram timeout prevents duplicate post across devices and restarts. DST gap scheduling fails; DST overlap chooses offset and persists exact UTC; timezone change never shifts frozen reservation. Scheduler missed time blocks rather than publishing silently. Native approval uses actual image/video, not text card. Reconnecting account requires explicit resume of blocked schedules.

Dependency-aware implementation roadmap

This is the plan for a separate implementation team; no application implementation is authorized by this design task.

1. Preserve existing data and harden foundation: production migrations/backups, account auth/workspace permissions, revision/idempotency/error contracts, immutable approval model; adopt tokens/components and router/native navigation. Gate: no cross-account resource access; existing carousel approval/publishing tests preserved. 2. Source/queue UX: paginated topics, saved/angle/source/claim contracts, daily schedule/worker-health UI, actual source provenance. Gate: old daily result preserved while latest refresh displays correct warnings; inaccessible sources not fabricated; manual evidence explicit verification. 3. Carousel editor: protected local buffer, CAS conflict recovery, stable IDs/order/history, freeform brief, stale composition/validation states, actual iOS artwork preview. Gate: no unsaved data loss, 6–8 current1080×1350 outputs; no template overlay fallback. 4. Reel vertical pipeline and editor: capability adapters, script/storyboard/assets/audio/subtitles/render/validate with per-unit persistence, desktop timeline and focused iOS editing. Gate: real decoded current1080×1920 30–40s MP4 and audio/cue validation, failures preserve units, never storyboard-ready-video confusion. 5. Independent review and revision requests: persisted human checklist per revision, format-specific approval, requested-change target/comments without auto-generation. Gate: edited format invalidates approval, shared evidence dependency invalidation, native actual-media review. 6. Instagram account OAuth/preflight and reservations/calendar: explicit immutable version/account/time authorization, atomic schedule claim/cancel, unknown-outcome reconciliation. Gate: designated test account verification by authorized operator; current fake tests do not establish live success; duplicate final calls prevented under timeout/crash. 7. Library lifecycle, jobs/notifications, analytics: archive/trash/version export, server heartbeat/cancel, deduplicated in-app/push, real account metrics and export metadata. Gate: missing metrics not zero, push denial nonblocking, secret-free exports, no active reservation purge. 8. End-to-end and release validation: run A–O acceptance/control tests with web keyboard/reflow and iOS VoiceOver/Dynamic Type/offline/two-device races; production backup/restore/retention monitoring; verify daily operations over multiple days, provider spend/quality and native packaging. Release only after no P0 workflow/access/data-loss/publication defects.

Engineering ownership: web React+Vite, local state Jotai and server query layer; native Expo/React Native upgrade plus native components/secure storage; Python FastAPI services, PostgreSQL production durable state, server workers/providers; API-first contracts before UI actions. UX should not pick cloud pricing, billing plans, live provider access or Meta app approval on engineering’s behalf.

Required creative-autopilot dependency

Before advertising varied ten-day Instagram output, implement server creative history/reservations,automatic concept planning,full-composition image/video provider generation,similarity validation and budget-scoped alternate concepts. This depends on immutable asset provenance,account-context history and durable per-unit jobs. Validate PC-AI01..09 with a seeded recent/scheduled10postcorpus, near/exactduplicate controls and empty-history case. UI theme tokens never become a mandatory content template. Do not gate delivery on manual art direction.

AUTH-01 — Welcome & sign in

Purpose: Authenticate without exposing provider credentials. Web route `/sign-in`; iOS destination `DevAI/AUTH-01`. Parent: Public; preserve invoker on reusable editor/preview.

Composition

Desktop: Centered 440px form and editorial welcome, no authenticated shell. At1440px use232px sidebar,32px page gutter and24px gaps; dedicated editors suppress ordinary shell content navigation but retain Done, entity title and Activity status.

iOS: Single scroll form, Apple system button, keyboard email, no tabs. Physical viewport390×844 with top safe area47 and bottom34; content scrolls between navigation and fixed actions. Body17pt,44pt targets. Native child editor hides tab bar and uses Done/Back; root destinations keep five tabs. Long form continuation is scrolling content, not a tall fictional phone.

Content and focus order: Email address → Continue with email → Continue with Apple → Privacy → Help. Read boundary: Proposed /v1 resource matching exact operation index; public auth requires no workspace read. Only the contracts below mutate. All IDs are stable, not row indices.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/AUTH-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/AUTH-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: AUTH-01-AT01, AUTH-01-AT02, AUTH-01-AT03, AUTH-01-AT04. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/AUTH-02-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/AUTH-02-default.svg`. Per-control tests: AUTH-02-AT01, AUTH-02-AT02, AUTH-02-AT03, AUTH-02-AT04. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting, empty, skeleton. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/QUEUE-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/QUEUE-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: QUEUE-01-AT01, QUEUE-01-AT02, QUEUE-01-AT03, QUEUE-01-AT04, QUEUE-01-AT05, QUEUE-01-AT06. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

URL-only submission (new capability)

At top choose 'URL idea' or 'Reviewed excerpt'. Reviewed excerpt is the existing POST /topics path and requires240–10000characters. URL idea submits only canonicalHTTPS to proposed POST /v1/ideas `{url,category,priority}` and returns ideaId/jobId; does not pretend a fetched article is verified. The extraction panel remains in this route with query `?idea=:ideaId`, stages Queued→Fetching→Excerpt ready or Access denied/Unsupported page/Failed. Original form buffer persists while extraction runs. User reviews captured excerpt, publisher identity and publication date, corrects text, then explicit Add topic creates reviewed source/topic. If extraction cannot access page, switch to Reviewed excerpt and paste legitimately obtained text; no fabricated summary. Duplicate opens existing topic. Cancel request is checkpoint cancellation and preserves URL. No provider generation or publishing from submitting an idea.

Controls: submission-mode radio local; extract-url POST /v1/ideas; idea-progress GET /v1/ideas/{ideaId}; confirm-excerpt POST /v1/ideas/{ideaId}/confirm `{expectedRevision,title,source,excerpt,publishedAt?,category,priority,humanReviewed:true}`; cancel-extraction POST /v1/jobs/{jobId}/cancel. Completion returns topicId, opens TOPIC-01. Keyboard and VoiceOver read extraction stage and captured date; unavailable source never receives a truth badge. Extra acceptance cases PC-URL01/02 below.

CREATE-01 — Choose format

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting, validation_error, unsaved, autosaving, saved, save_failed, conflict. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/CREATE-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/CREATE-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: CREATE-01-AT01, CREATE-01-AT02, CREATE-01-AT03, CREATE-01-AT04, CREATE-01-AT05. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

Latest user requirement — AI autonomy and varied content

Authoritative: handoff/ai-creative-autopilot.md. No required visual brief/layout/background input. AI creates whole compositions and Reel assets automatically and checks recent/scheduled visual history. Regenerate chooses a fresh AI direction; prior assets retained. App UI palette does not constrain Instagram artwork. Full Reel must be validated MP4.

Fast default flow

The only required user choices are topic and format. Continue saves default setup then goes directly to CREATE-03; Preferences opens CREATE-02 optionally. Defaults:8slides,35secondReel,English,clear editorial writing,AI autonomous visuals,subtitles on,voice automatic only if tested available (otherwise explicit No voice on summary),musicNone. Review displays all defaults and provider capability; Generate is the only paid authorization. CREATE-02 is an optional preferences screen, not a required request for creative ideas.

CREATE-01-EX6 optional-preferences: Open CREATE-02 optional preferences for saved setup; no layout prompt. Operation None: navigation; test CREATE-01-EX6-AT.

CREATE-01-EX7 choose-topic: Inline searchable topic picker uses GET /v1/topics; choose stable topicId, inspect sources, then format choices; no paid work. Operation GET /v1/topics?q=&cursor=; test CREATE-01-EX7-AT.

CREATE-01-EX8 custom-topic: Open IMPORT-01 with safe returnTo=/create; confirmed topic returns to format selection. Operation None: navigation; test CREATE-01-EX8-AT.

Library Create without topic: show the source-backed topic picker before format choices; empty picker offers Add custom topic. Pick topic does not alter the editorial queue selected topic. Confirmed import safely returns topicId to this setup. Do not generate with undefined/missing topic evidence.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting, queued, running, partial_success, stalled, cancelling, cancelled, completed, retryable_failure, permanent_failure. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/GEN-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/GEN-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: GEN-01-AT01, GEN-01-AT02, GEN-01-AT03, GEN-01-AT04, GEN-01-AT05, GEN-01-AT06. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

App UI palette does not constrain Instagram artwork. Full Reel must be validated MP4.

GEN-01-EX7 different-look: Confirm budget; AI chooses different concept/background, preserves prior assets and enqueues scope-specific generation. Operation POST /v1/outputs/{outputId}/different-look; test GEN-01-EX7-AT.

GEN-01-EX8 view-comparison: Read-only comparison with nearest recent/scheduled output, date and reason; close restores focus. Operation GET /v1/outputs/{outputId}/creative-plan; test GEN-01-EX8-AT.

Composition

Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/VER-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/VER-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: VER-01-AT01, VER-01-AT02, VER-01-AT03, VER-01-AT04. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting, validation_error, unsaved, autosaving, saved, save_failed, conflict. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/REEL-04-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/REEL-04-default.svg`. Per-control tests: REEL-04-AT01, REEL-04-AT02, REEL-04-AT03, REEL-04-AT04, REEL-04-AT05, REEL-04-AT06. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

Latest user requirement — AI autonomy and varied content

Authoritative: handoff/ai-creative-autopilot.md. No required visual brief/layout/background input. AI creates whole compositions and Reel assets automatically and checks recent/scheduled visual history. Regenerate chooses a fresh AI direction; prior assets retained. App UI palette does not constrain Instagram artwork. Full Reel must be validated MP4.

REEL-05 — Voiceover & music

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/PUB-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/PUB-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: PUB-01-AT01, PUB-01-AT02, PUB-01-AT03, PUB-01-AT04, PUB-01-AT05, PUB-01-AT06, PUB-01-AT07, PUB-01-AT08. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting, empty, skeleton. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

State and persistence contract

Applicable states: initial, loading, populated, failure, permission_denied, session_expired, offline, reconnecting, validation_error, unsaved, autosaving, saved, save_failed, conflict. Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/SET-03-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/SET-03-default.svg`. Per-control tests: SET-03-AT01, SET-03-AT02, SET-03-AT03, SET-03-AT04, SET-03-AT05, SET-03-AT06, SET-03-AT07. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

Authoritative daily editorial behavior

See handoff/product-scope-v3.md. Rolling last24hourdeveloper-focused research, all findings reachable, priority descending with source-backed explanations and explicit independent approval/publication. Earlier undated illustrative topics or broader recency filters must not appear as fresh daily news.

State and persistence contract

Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Loading reserves actual layout using skeletons for lists, inline spinner only for submitted control; empty list explains scope and offers Clear filters or relevant create action. Do not show an empty screen while loading. Offline exposes cached timestamp, queues only protected text edits, and disables generation, approval and publication with explanation. Session expiry keeps protected local buffer and returns here after authentication. Permission loss immediately removes mutation actions while preserving permitted read view.

Editors save800ms after idle, ordered per entity with expectedRevision. Saved requires ACK; failure retains buffer and Save now.409 displays field-level base/mine/server comparison; accepting server never silently deletes mine. Back/Done with unacknowledged buffer offers Save, Discard local changes, Stay; selecting Save must succeed before leaving. Navigation never cancels durable jobs. Read-only screens have no autosave, draft, or paid action merely because they share components.

Dialogs follow interactions/dialogs.md; per-control triggers and responses are in interactions.json. Native focus enters screen title; web route title receives focus except returning to original invoker. Preview revision is labeled and retained; latest revision refresh cannot silently swap an approved preview.

Visuals and tests

Desktop `visuals/desktop/SYS-01-default.svg`; iOS `visuals/ios/SYS-01-default.svg`. Per-control tests: SYS-01-AT01, SYS-01-AT02, SYS-01-AT03. Critical state-specific cases are in acceptance/production-cases.md.

SYS-02 — Not found

Purpose: Recover from deleted or unknown destinations. Web route `/not-found`; iOS destination `DevAI/SYS-02`. Parent: Original destination; preserve invoker on reusable editor/preview.

Composition

Desktop: 404/denied friendly page with requested route context, no sensitive entity title. At1440px use232px sidebar,32px page gutter and24px gaps; dedicated editors suppress ordinary shell content navigation but retain Done, entity title and Activity status.

iOS: Unavailable destination, native Back or safe Home. Physical viewport390×844 with top safe area47 and bottom34; content scrolls between navigation and fixed actions. Body17pt,44pt targets. Native child editor hides tab bar and uses Done/Back; root destinations keep five tabs. Long form continuation is scrolling content, not a tall fictional phone.

Content and focus order: Page unavailable → Back → Go home. Read boundary: Proposed /v1 resource matching exact operation index; public auth requires no workspace read. Only the contracts below mutate. All IDs are stable, not row indices.

Latest user requirement — AI autonomy and varied content

Authoritative: handoff/ai-creative-autopilot.md. No required visual brief/layout/background input. AI creates whole compositions and Reel assets automatically and checks recent/scheduled visual history. Regenerate chooses a fresh AI direction; prior assets retained. App UI palette does not constrain Instagram artwork. Full Reel must be validated MP4.

GEN-02-EX6 different-look: Confirm budget; AI chooses different concept/background, preserves prior assets and enqueues scope-specific generation. Operation POST /v1/outputs/{outputId}/different-look; test GEN-02-EX6-AT.

GEN-02-EX7 view-comparison: Read-only comparison with nearest recent/scheduled output, date and reason; close restores focus. Operation GET /v1/outputs/{outputId}/creative-plan; test GEN-02-EX7-AT.

Goal: inspect all research runs,source coverage,exact frozen24hourwindows and findings. Desktop route /discover/research; native Discover→Researchhistory;Back restores selectedrun/filter/scroll.

Active runs linkActivity andmayexposepartialverifiedfindings. Native groupedrunrows pushrankedfindings;Coverage openslargesheetwithsourceattemptlistandRetryfailedsources. Default newest runfirst,24rowstablecursorpagination.

Loading skeleton matchesrows;emptyhistory offersRunresearch;filteredempty offersClearfilters;readfailurekeepscachedrowswithasOftimestamp. Offlinecachedhistoryallowed,noresearch/retry. Failedsourcesretainedindependentlyofverifiedresults. Retryfixedwindowneverdriftswithcurrenttime. Cancellationkeepspartialfindings. Permission/sessionrecoveryusesSYS-01/SYS-02andneverexposesanotherworkspace'ssources.

All controls in interactions.json with exact next destinations. New contracts in handoff/product-scope-v3.md. AcceptancePC-R24-01..08 and controltests. Visuals desktop/ios DISC-02-default.svg; high-fidelity style followsvisual-quality-v3.md.
